      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Term labels and percentage compositions are assigned to energy levels based on a parametric fitting reproducing similar calculations of <a onmouseover="self.status='Bibliographic Reference for Tc I';return true;" onmouseout="self.status='';return true;" target="_blank" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="popded('https://physics.nist.gov/cgi-bin/ASBib1/get_ASBib_ref.cgi?db=el&amp;db_id=14280&amp;comment_code=&amp;element=Tc&amp;spectr_charge=1&amp;type=E');return false">Palmeri and Wyart 1999 </a>. Due to strong configuration mixing, these term labels do not fully reflect physical nature of the energy levels and are used for bookkeeping purpose only. The ionization energy is from <a onmouseover="self.status='Bibliographic Reference for Tc I';return true;" onmouseout="self.status='';return true;" target="_blank" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="popded('https://physics.nist.gov/cgi-bin/ASBib1/get_ASBib_ref.cgi?db=el&amp;db_id=15237&amp;comment_code=&amp;element=Tc&amp;spectr_charge=1&amp;type=E');return false">Mattolat et al. 2010 </a>.
